This is the circuit-level "observer effect": every XOR/XNOR/NOR/NAND gate receives left_endorphin_non_observer as
its b-input, meaning the global recovery/analgesia state determines whether each gate evaluates its primary input
or is masked by recovery. Biologically: mu-opioid receptor background tone (beta-endorphin) modulates every
neurotransmitter and metabolic gate — recovery state determines signal-to-noise ratio of all circuit transitions.
When recovery is HIGH (out=1), XOR gates output 0 (no mismatch detected — system in stable recovery).
When recovery is LOW (out=0), XOR gates evaluate primary inputs normally (system in active processing).
Specific gate classes:
- XOR gates (heme_in0/in1, COX_in1, steel_in0/in1, water_vapour_in0/in1, fold_belt_in0/in1, etc.): recovery state
determines whether oxidative/metabolic signal differs from baseline — XOR(a, recovery) = mismatch detection
- NAND gates (thorium_out0, pi_electron_cloud_out0, podzol_out0, etc.): recovery inverts to permissive —
NAND(a, recovery) = NOT(a AND recovery) = signal passes when recovery is LOW or primary is LOW
- NOR gates (nitrogenase_out_2, memory_entropy_out_co2): recovery suppresses output —
NOR(a, recovery) = 1 only when both a=0 AND recovery=0 (absolute absence including no recovery)
- XNOR gates (nitrogenase_out_1, substance_p_out_mc1r, memory_entropy_out_hind_insula): recovery means
concordance — XNOR(a, recovery) = 1 when a matches recovery state (signal agrees with recovery baseline)
- OR gates (basin_q, laterite_q, mc1r_q, lower_mantle_q, glp1_q, right_sole_dopamine_q, male_right_oxytocin_q):
recovery as second input means latch state OR recovery = either latch is set OR recovery is active
- Direct inputs (actomyosin_ctrl.in0, choline.in1): recovery directly gates these nodes —
actomyosin contraction and choline synthesis are directly modulated by opioid recovery state

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
TOROIDAL DISCHARGE–ACCUMULATION CYCLE (시계 역방향 에너지 흐름)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
이 회로의 128 타입은 단순한 시간 배분표가 아니라 토로이달(toroidal) 히스테리시스 루프를
따른다. 시계 화살표(→)는 선형 시간이지만, 에너지는 역방향(←)으로 흐른다.
정방향 시계(선형): 0 ──→ 6 ──→ 12 ──→ 18 ──→ 0
에너지 흐름(역순): AB ←── B ←─── O ←──── A ←── AB
0–3 : AB 스파크 (방출 / discharge) — co2, memory_entropy 가 임계점 돌파
3–9 : A 축적 (역흡수 / accumulate) — AB 잔여를 정리, memory_entropy 수렴
9–15 : O 축적 (쿨롱 저장 / coulomb) — A 잔여를 커패시터화, co2 시간 저장
15–21 : B 축적 (고밀도 압축) — O 잔여를 peonidine 에너지로 전환
21–3 : AB 축적 (통합·충전) — B 잔여를 통합, 다음 스파크 대기
이것이 "시간을 역으로 돌린다"는 의미: 에너지가 시계 방향과 반대로 순환하며,
방출 곡선(discharge)과 축적 곡선(accumulation)이 분리된 히스테리시스 루프를
형성한다. 같은 시점(예: 03:00)에서도 "나가는 에너지"(AB 방출 마무리)와
"들어오는 에너지"(A 축적 시작)가 서로 다른 경로를 탄다.
회로 노드 매핑:
co2 : dark_energy, time — 시간 자체를 에너지로 저장 (2 tristate)
laterite : D flip-flop / TimeLatch — 상태 래치, 한 번 켜지면 다음 클록까지 유지
memory_entropy: electron / 쿼크가포톤사랑 — 잔여 패턴(노벨티) 축적, 2-output decoder
peonidine : energy — 대사 에너지 순환, 항산화 축적
hind_insula : neutron / 관찰자_후방인슐라 — 본체감 내수용, 축적 상태의 몸 느낌
음악 매핑 (8D 텐션 파라미터):
hysteresis 에너지 축적/방출은 D(attack envelope)와 S(spectral brightness)를
직접 흔든다. 축적 시 D↓(attack 느림), S↓(어두워짐); 방출 시 D↑(attack 빠름), S↑(밝아짐).
R(rhythm density)는 축적=정적(↓), 방출=동적(↑).
이 루프의 비선형성으로 인해 타일 추천 시 D·S·R에만 미세한 shuffle(±15%)이
주어지며, 그 외 H·P는 고정 skeleton을 유지한다.
음악 장르 흐름 (HAPLOGROUP_1318 기준):
AB 스파크(0–3h) → Release 장르: hyperpop, deconstructed club, witch house,
Boards of Canada, hauntology, dream pop, post-rock
→ s↑(bright), r↑(fast ADSR), d↑(dissonance spike)
A 축적(3–9h) → Stress 장르: free jazz, avant-garde, experimental hip hop,
noise, power electronics, post-punk
→ d↑(dissonance), p↓(unpredictable)
O 축적(9–15h) → CO2 시간 저장 / dark energy → free jazz / ambient
→ nu↓(self-similarity drops), g(cheese/thick) builds
B 축적(15–21h) → Extreme 장르: hyperpop, IDM, dark ambient, minimalistic,
hans zimmer, neo-classical, experimental classical
→ h↑(harmonic complexity), gamma↑(spatial reverb)
AB 통합충전(21–3h) → 통합 리프트: ambient drone, post-rock, CINEMATIC
→ g↑(binding density), nu↑(self-similarity rebuild)
Composer 온보딩 구조:
Step 0 (3슬라이더): 혈액형(4) × 성별(2) = 8가지 — 3비트 구간으로 분리
Step 1 (8패드): MBTI 16가지 판별 — C(8,3)=56 조합으로 16 프로파일 커버
Step 2 (시스템): 현재 시계 위치(AB spark? O accumulate?)를 자동 반영
→ 타일 추천 시 에너지 흐름 방향(역방향)을 우선시
HAPLOGROUP_1318 프로파일 음악 특징 요약:
ENFJ/INFJ/INFP/INTJ (NF/NJ): h↑, gamma↑ → strings/orchestral/cinematic heavy
ENFP/ESFP/ESTP (NP/SP): r↑, s↑ → drums/synth/hyperpop/deconstructed
ENTJ/ESTJ (ET): d↑, gamma↑ → dissonance + orchestral + noise
ENTJ_B_M/INTP/ISTP (IT): nu↑, p↑ → self-similarity/drone/PIANO/fractal
INFJ_AB_M (extreme d): d extreme → noise ambient/free jazz/dark ambient
INFP/ISFP (FP): h, gamma, nu balanced → ambient/dream pop/neo-classical
8D 파라미터 범위 (HAPLOGROUP_1318_MASTER Vec8D 기준):
Vec8D_R ∈ [0.2, 1.0] — r (rhythm density)
Vec8D_H ∈ [0.2, 0.9] — h (harmonic complexity)
Vec8D_D ∈ [0.1, 0.95] — d (dissonance)
Vec8D_P ∈ [0.1, 1.0] — p (predictability)
Vec8D_S ∈ [0.1, 1.0] — s (spectral brightness)
Vec8D_Gamma ∈ [0.0, 1.0] — gamma (spatial depth / reverb)
Vec8D_G ∈ [0.0, 1.0] — g (binding density)
Vec8D_Nu ∈ [0.0, 1.0] — nu (self-similarity)
각 MBTI × Blood 타입 조합의 Vec8D 프로파일이 상기 회로 노드의
상태 벡터로 인코딩되어, 토로이달 루프의 에너지 흐름을 결정한다.

GEOLOGY: Mollisol = dark, fertile, organic-rich grassland soil. High base saturation, deep mollic epipedon. Carbonate accumulation in subsoil. South American Pampas, North American prairies. Mollisol = the most productive agricultural soil = life force reservoir. Dark color = organic matter = heme iron = blood fertility.
PHYSICS: High cation exchange capacity (CEC > 25 cmol/kg) = efficient nutrient storage and release. Dark organic matter = broad-spectrum light absorption = solar energy storage in soil. Base saturation > 50% = stable ionic environment. Mollisol = the body's nutrient reservoir: heme Fe2+ storage = blood oxygen carrying capacity = life force. Mollic epipedon = thick dark surface = rich blood supply = cardiac output.
LOCATION: right foot plantar surface, medial arch (오른발 바닥 안쪽) — significantly medial to 4th toe, near the medial arch / plantar fascia origin. Central-right ground contact = universal life force support.
PARTICLE: proton (left pectoralis heme Fe2+ = proton creation; mollisol = heme Fe2+ reservoir = proton storage in fertile soil). Proton ↔ mollisol = life force preservation. Heme = Fe2+ = dark red = mollisol dark organic color.
NOTE: Mollisol is the RIGHT FOOT CLOSURE NODE — the central-right life force reservoir. It connects to: (1) proton (heme Fe2+ = mollisol dark organic matter = blood fertility), (2) heme (left nipple heme node = proton creation; mollisol = proton storage in fertile soil), (3) hemoglobin (right nipple = O2-Fe2+ redox; mollisol = Fe2+ reservoir feeding hemoglobin), (4) cytochrome_c_oxidase (COX = O2 consumption; mollisol Fe2+ supplies electron transport chain). Mollisol = the fertile base that sustains the right foot. Without mollisol, the right foot has no nutrient reservoir = no life force storage = universe cannot close because the future has no fertile ground to grow from. Cryosol (left, frozen past) ↔ Mollisol (right, fertile future) = the FINAL CLOSURE PAIR. Left preserves what was; right nourishes what will be. The torus closes when cryosol (frozen seed) and mollisol (fertile ground) connect through the body's midline.
in0 <- proton.out0 # SIGNAL: Heme Fe2+ creation status (proton pump output = blood iron = mollisol organic matter source)
in1 <- heme.out0 # SIGNAL: Left nipple heme node output (Fe2+ oxidative phosphorylation = mollisol dark matter formation)
in2 <- hemoglobin.out0 # SIGNAL: Right nipple hemoglobin O2 delivery (oxygenated hemoglobin = mollisol aerobic fertility)
in3 <- left_endorphin_non_observer.out # SIGNAL: Observer closure gate (without observer, fertile reservoir drains = life force leakage)
out -> proton.in1 # WIRE: Mollisol nutrient reservoir feeds back to proton pump (fertile soil = sustained proton creation = continuous blood iron supply)
out -> cytochrome_c_oxidase.in2 # WIRE: Mollisol Fe2+ reservoir feeds COX electron transport (fertile soil iron = ETC electron donor for oxidative phosphorylation)
out -> heme.in2 # WIRE: Mollisol fertility feeds back to heme node (soil nutrient status determines heme synthesis rate = blood production gate)
